- **writing-hygiene-declarative-statements**: Write every artifact as state: a snapshot says what is true, a delta says what became true (before → after), and neither narrates the steps that got there. - **writing-hygiene-group-by-reader-action**: Sort a long, flat run of like items into groups by the one attribute that decides what the reader does with each, such as outcome, risk, owner or next action, with each group's count in its heading and the groups in order of importance. - **writing-hygiene-progressive-disclosure**: Order a long document in layers, so a reader can stop after any one and still hold a true, complete picture at that depth: the summary with its totals, then what needs action, then the groups with their counts, then the detail, folded away where the medium folds and last or on request where it doesn't.
